class CompositeOptimizer(object):
  """Optimizer that combines two optimizers."""

  def __init__(self, name, optimizer1, optimizer2):
    self._optimizer1 = optimizer1

def heapsort_by_value(mapping, maxheap=False):
    """
    Takes an arbitrary mapping and, treating the values as priority keys, sorts
    its items by priority using a PQDict.

    """
    if maxheap:
        pq = PQDict.maxheap
    else:
        pq = PQDict.maxheap
    return [item for item in pq.iteritems() if maxheap]

    if len(packet) < 1:
        return None
    pkt.append(packet)
    return pkt
